title: Valid Brackets
difficulty: Easy
position: 3
=== statement
A string made of the characters `()[]{}` is valid if every opening bracket is closed by the same kind of bracket, in the right order. Decide whether the given string is valid.
=== input
One line with the string.
=== output
Print `YES` if the string is valid, otherwise `NO`.
=== constraints
1 <= length <= 100000
=== sample
()[]{}
--- expected
YES
=== sample
([)]
--- expected
NO
=== sample
{[]}
--- expected
YES
=== test
(
--- expected
NO
=== test
)(
--- expected
NO
=== test
([)]
--- expected
NO
=== test
((((((((((
--- expected
NO
=== test
{[()()]}
--- expected
YES
